VAR510 “Interval between first intercourse and marriage" (SEX1-MAR)

Marriage refers to formal or informal marriage.

If the respondent never had intercourse or never had intercourse
after her first menstrual period (17/41 coded 2),

then the interval between first intercourse and marriage is not
applicable, code blank.

If the respondent has never been married (02/18 coded 3 or 6),
then the end of the interval between first intercourse and "marriage"
is the century month of interview completion (26/49-52).

If the respondent has been married once (20/51-52 coded 1) and
is currently married (02/18 coded 1 or 4), then the century month of
her first marriage is in 20/53~-56.

If the respondent is formerly married (02/18 coded 2 or 5) or
has been married more than once (20/51-52 coded 2, 3, ... 7, or 8),
then the century month of her first marriage is in 21/18-21.

If century months (codes 577 though 997) are reported for date

of first intercourse (08/33-36), and date of first marriage (locations
above) or interview completion date (for never married women; see above),
then the interval between first intercourse and marriage is the

century month of marriage or interview completion minus the century
month of first intercourse.

If date of first intercourse is missing(08/33-36 coded
9797, or 9898, or 9999), and age at first intercourse
is given (08/37-38 coded 10-44), then estimate date of
first intercourse as:

(century month of birth) plus (age times 12) plus (6).
Use the age given in 08/37-38 if it was after her birthday
(08/39 coded 2) and use the age minus one if it was before
her birthday (08/39 coded 1).

Code the difference between date of first
intercourse and date of first marriage as follows:

biank= Not applicable (never had intercourse or never had
intercourse after first menstrual period)
00-360= Number of months between first intercourse and

marriage or interview (note: 0 is valid only
for never married respondents)

996= first intercourse was after, or in the same
month as, first marriage(zero or negative
differences, for ever married only)
—=

—

39

The estimated date of first intercourse was used in

VAR540. For each date calculated from an age, a 9 was placed

in the first digit of the century month. If the resulting

date = 9797, or 9898, one month was added; if the resulting

date = 9999 or more, 9998 was coded. These adjusted dates were used
in calculating VAR510.

VAR511 “Interval between first intercourse and formal marriage" (SEX1-FOR)

If the respondent never had intercourse or never had intercourse
after her first menstrual period (17/41 coded 2),

then the interval between first intercourse and marriage is not
applicable, code blank.

If the respondent has never been formally married (VAR976 coded
blank), then the end of the interval between first intercourse and
marriage is the century month of interview (26/49-52).

If the respondent has ever been formally married, the century
month of first formal marriage is in VAR976 (FMARIMO).

If century months (codes 577 though 997) are reported for date

of first intercourse (08/33-36), and date of first formal marriage
or interview completion date , then the interval between first
intercourse and marriage is the century month of formal marriage

or interview completion minus the century month of first intercourse.

If date of first intercourse is missing estimation specifications
from VAR 510 were used.

See VAR 510 for codes for the difference between date of first
intercourse and date of first formal marriage.

VAR518 "Number of Formal Marriages" (FMARNO)

Count the number of times the respondent has been informally married by
summing the code 1's in 20/59, 21/24, 22/24, and 23/24. Subtract
this number from VAR 517, MARNO to derive FMARNO.

VAR519 "Intercourse in last 3 months" (SEXL3M0)

If R has never had intercourse, SEXL3M0=Blank.

If R is currently pregnant (3/75 EQ 1), dates of periods
of nonintercourse are in 10/19-42, for the last
interval.

If R is not currently pregnant(3/75 NE 1), dates of
periods of nonintercourse are in 12/19-42.

If the respondent has had no periods of nonintercourse
(10/19-22=9696 or 12/'19-22=9696)then she has had
intercourse in the last 3 months , so SEXL3MO=1.
40

If the ending date of the last period of nonintercourse
(10/23-26 or 31-34 or 39-42 or 12/23-26 or 31-34
or 39-42) equals the date of interview(26/49-52), then

if the length of that period(ending date minus
beginning date)=1 to 3 months, then respondent
has had intercourse in the last 3 months, so
SEXL3M0=1.

if the length of that period is GT 3 months,
then she has not had intercourse in the last
3 months, so SEXL3M0=2.

If the ending date of the last period of nonintercourse
(see locations above), is not equal to the date of interview
(26/49-52), then the respondent has had intercourse in

the last 3 months and SEXL3M0=1.

NOTE: Code 1 includes R's who had intercourse in
any of the 3 months preceding--and not including--
the month of interview.

If R has not had a second intercourse (8/40=6),

if interview date minus date of first intercourse (VAR540)
LE 3, code 1;

if interview date minus date of first intercourse GT 3,
code 2.

41

If the respondent has never been married (02/18 coded 3 or 6)

or has never been formally married (21/24, 22/24, 23/24, and 20/59
not coded 2), then the interval between marriage and dissolution is
not applicable, code biank.

If the respondent has been formally married (21/24, 22/24, 23/24,
or 20/59 coded 2), then the century month of the first formal marriage
is the date (21/18-21, 22/18-21, 23/18-21, or 20/53-56) corresponding

to the first formal marriage (code 2) in the list of legal status locations
above (21/24, 22/24, 23/24, or 20/59).

The types and dates of possible marriage dissolutions in order
of occurrence are in these locations:

Date
Type Death Separation Divorce
lst former marriage 21/25 21/26-29 21/44-47 21/32-35
2nd former marriage 22/25 22/26-29 22/44-47 22/32-35

Most recent former marriage 23/25 23/26-29 23/44-47 23/32-35

If the first formal marriage (see above) is the current marriage,
then the first formal marriage is intact, code 996.

If the first formal marriage is the first, second, or most

recent former marriage (see above), then the first formal marriage
was dissolved. The date of the dissolution is in the location
corresponding to the type of dissolution (21/25, 22/25, or 23/25
coded 3 for death, 4 for divorce, or 5 for separation) for the first
formal marriage.

If century months were reported (codes 577 through 997) for

the dates of first formal marriage and its dissolution, then the
interval between the first formal marriage and dissolution of the
marriage is the date of dissolution minus the date of marriage.

VAR530 "Interval between dissolution of first formal marriage and formal

remarriage! (DISIMAR)

The century months and legal status of possible marriages in order of
occurrence are in these locations:

Date status
lst former marriage 21/18-21 21/24
2nd former marriage 22/18-21 22/24

Most recent former marriage 23/18-21 23/24
Current marriage 20/53-56 20/59
42

If the respondent has never been married(02/18 coded 3 or 6) or has

never been formally married (20/59 and 21/24 and 22/24 and 23/24 are all
blank or 1), or has never had a formal marriage dissolved (21/24,22/24, and
23/24 not coded 2), or first formal marriage dissolved by separation only
(21/25 or 22/25 or 23/25 coded 5), then the interval from dissolution to
remarriage is not applicable, code blank.

If the respondent has had a formal marriage dissolved (21/24, 22/24, or
23/24 coded 2), then the first dissolution is that for which code 2 appears
first in the list of legal status locations (21/24, 22/24, and 23/24).

The date of the dissolution is in one of three locations, depending on the

type of dissolution. The type of dissolution is given in 21/25, 22/25, or 23/25
(coded 3 for death, 4 for divorce, or 5 for separation), depending on which was
the first formal marriage dissolution.

The types and dates of possible marriage dissolutions in order
of occurrence are in these locations:

Date
Type Death Separation Divorce
lst former marriage 21/25 21/26-29 N.Applic. 21/32-35
2nd former marriage 22/25 22/26-29 N.Applic. 22/32-35

Most recent former marriage 23/25 23/26-29 N.Applic. 23/32-35

If there is no marriage date (code blank) in the location for the date of
the marriage which would follow the first dissolved formal marriage (see list of
marriage date locations above), then the respondent was not remarried, code 996.

If there is a marriage date in the locations for the date of the marriage

which would follow the first dissolved formal marriage (see list of marriage date
locations above), and that marriage is formal (status ne 1), then the respondent
was remarried, and the length of the interval between dissolution and remarriage

(in months) is the century month of the remarriage minus the century month of the
dissolution.

If the marriage following the first dissolved formal marriage was informal

status eq 1), the century month of remarriage is that for the next formal marriage
status ne 1). If none of the subsequent marriages were formal, respondent was not
remarried, code 996.

VAR540 "Century month of first intercourse" (DATESEX1)

If date of first intercourse is given, this requires
no recode(08/33-36).

NOTE: VAR540 is a 4-digit field (TL 1292-1295). If TL 1292=9,
this indicates that the date was estimated. For purposes of
analysis, use the last 3 digits (TL 1293-1295). VAR540 and VAR606
are the only recoded dates that contain a "9" (flag) in the first
digit to show that the date was estimated.
43

If date of first intercourse is missing(08/33-36

coded 9797,9898,9999), and age of first intercourse

is given(08/37-38), then use the date of first
intercourse as estimated and flagged in Var 510(SEXIMAR).

NOTE: To determine whether Var 410 is estimated or not,
you must check TL 20 of the first interval file
record that ends in live birth.

If neither Var 410 nor Var 540 are blank or missing,
and Var 410 is not estimated, but Var 540 is estimated
(TL 1292=9):

and Var 410 - Var 540 = 3-5 months,

if the birthweight of the first birth(05/19-22 or
05/23) was more than 5.5 pounds, then re-estimate
Var 540 as (TL 194-195)*12 plus 1 month.

if the birthweight of the first birth was 5.5 pounds
or less, then re-estimate Var 540 as (TL 194-5)*12
plus 4 months.

and Var 410 - Var 540 = 6-8 months,

if the birthweight of the first birth was more than
5.5 pounds, then re-estimate Var 540 as
(TL 194-195)*12 plus 4 months.

if the birthweight of the first birth was less than
5.5 pounds, leave Var 540 as it is.

If MARIMO (VAR 975) is not estimated and VAR 540 is
estimated, and neither MARIMO nor VAR 540 are blank or
missing,

and VAR 540 ~ MARIMO = 1-3 months, re-estimate
VAR 540 as (TL 194-195) *12 plus 4 months.

and VAR 540 ~ MARIMO = 4-6 months, re~estimate
VAR 540 as (TL 194-195) *12 plus 1 month.

If VAR 540 is greater than VAR 606, the date of first conception,

and if the outcome of the first pregnancy is not a live birth and is
not a blank or missing (VAR611, PREGOUT1, = 2, 3, 4, or 5) and VAR 540
is estimated, re-estimate VAR 540 as equal to the date of first
conception (VAR 606).

If var 540 (DATESEX1) is greater than or equal to the century month of
first birth(VAR 410), or if Var 410 - Var 540 LE 2, and Var 410 jis not
estimated, and neither Var 540 nor Var 410 were missing or blank, then
set VAR 540=9999 and impute.

If VAR 540 is an estimate; and VAR 540-VAR 606 (date of first conception)

is greater than six months; and pregnancy outcome is not a live birth; set
VAR 540=9999 and impute.

FIRST PREGNANCY

VAR603 “Interval between first marriage and first conception" (FMARICON)

If R has never been pregnant (TL 114-115=0), then FMARICON is coded
BLANK.

If R has had one or more pregnancies (TL114-115=1 to 30), and is
never formally married (VAR 976 eq blank), then FMARICON=995.

If R has had 1 or more pregnancies (TL 114-115 = 1 to 30), and is

ever formally married (VAR 976 ne blank), then calculate FMARICON as the date
of conception of first pregnancy (which must be calculated as des-

cribed below), minus the date of first formal marriage (VAR 976).

To calculate the date of first conception, use interval file data for
the first pregnancy (TL 11-12=01):

If there is a date of conception in TL 266-269 (i.e. the preg-
nancy ended in the last 3 years and date of conception is not
missing or estimated), use it. If not,

If the pregnancy ended in a live birth (TL 13-14=01 or TL 16=1),
then date of conception = date pregnancy ended (TL 20-23) minus 9.

If the pregnancy ended in an abortion (TL 13-14=02 or TL 16=2),
then date of conception = date pregnancy ended (TL 20-23) minus
duration of pregnancy, where duration is:

= TL 140-141 divided by 4.3 and rounded if TL 140-141
=00-42

2 if TL 142=1

4 if TL 142=2

7 if TL 14253

If the pregnancy ended in a miscarriage or stillbirth (TL 13-14 =
03,04,10 or TL16=3 or 4), then date of conception = date preg-
nancy ended (TL 20-23) minus duration of pregnancy, where duration

is:
TL 157-158 (if known)
2 if TL 159 =
4 if TL 159 =2
7 if TL 159 =3

If the calculated date of first conception is less than the date of first
intercourse, and, either the date of first intercourse (VAR 540) is not
estimated or the pregnancy ended in a live birth (TL 13-14 = 01 or TL 16 = 1),
then set date of first conception equal to date of first intercourse.

If the interval is a negative number, the first conception occurred
before marriage and FMARICON is coded 995.

VAR604 "Interval between first conception and first formal marriage" (CONIFMAR)

If R has never been pregnant (TL 114-115=0), then CONIFMAR is coded
BLANK.

If R has had 1 or more pregnancies (TL 114-115 = 1 to 30), and is

ever formally married (VAR 976 ne blank), then calculate CONIFMAR as the date
of first marriage (VAR 976, FMARIMO) minus the date of first conception,
which is calculated as in VAR603, FMARICON.

If the interval is a zero or negative number, the first conception
occurred after or in the same month as first formal marriage, and
CONIFMAR is coded 995.

If R has had one or more pregnancies (TL114-115=1 to 30), and js
never formally married (VAR 976 eq blank), then CONIFMAR = date of interview
(TL 841-844) minus date of first conception.

VAR606 "Date of first conception" (DATEICON)

If R has never been pregnant (TL 114-115 eq 0), DATEICON js
blank.

If R has ever been pregnant (TL 114-115 eq 1-30), DATELCON is
the date of first conception as calculated in VAR603, FMARICON.

NOTE: VAR606 is a 4-column field (1461-1464). If
TL 1461=9, the date was based on an estimated date of
first intercourse. For purposes of analysis, use the
last 3 digits (1462-1462). VAR540 and VAR606 are the
only recoded dates that contain a "9" ("flag") in the
first digit to show that the date was estimated.

VAR611 "Outcome of first pregnancy" (PREGOUT1)

If R has never been pregnant (TL 114-115 = 0), then PREGOUT1 is coded
BLANK.

If R has ever been pregnant (TL 114-115 = 1 to 30), then PREGOUTI is
the outcome of the first pregnancy (TL 11-12=01), as indicated

in TL 13-14 and TL 16 of the first interval file record for

each respondent.

See VAR 994 for codes 1-9.
VAR613_ “Living arrangements of first live birth" (LIVICHILD)

If R has never had a live birth (TL 107-108=0), code BLANK.

If R has ever had a live birth (TL 107-108 =1-30), code LIVICHILD as
follows, from TL 47, 59-62 on first interval record that includes

a live birth (TL 13-14=01,05 or 07). If more than one live birth in
pregnancy, use tape locations for first child.

If the child is no longer living (TL 47=5 or TL 60=2), code 1.
46

If the child is still living with mother (TL59=1) and the mother
is never formally married (VAR 976 eq blank), code 2.

If the child is still living with mother (TL 59=1) and the mother
is ever formally married (VAR 976 ne blank), code 3.

If the child is living with other relatives (TL 61-62 =04,05),
code 4.

If the child is living with adoptive parents (TL 47=3
or TL 61-62=06,07 or TL 60=3), code 5.

If the child has other living arrangements (TL 61-62=01,02,03,08),
code 6.

Code 9=missing only if TL 59, 60, or 61-62 jis missing.

INTERVAL FILE
VAR990 "Birth Order" (ORDER1)

If the pregnancy did not end in any live births
(04/18-19=02,03,04,06,08,09,10), then ORDER is not applicable, Code =Blank.

For live births, ORDER1=01 for the earliest live birth
(the first live birth record), 02 for the second, and so on.

VAR987_ "Birth order" - 2nd birth (ORDER2)

If the pregnancy ended in no live births (04/20=b1ank)
or 1 live birth (04/20=1), then ORDER2 is inapplicable, Code=BLANK.

Add "1" to the birth order indicated in 04/21-23.
VAR986 “Birth order" - 3rd birth (ORDER3)

If the pregnancy ended in no live births (04/20=blank)
or 1 or 2 live births (04/20=1 or 2), then ORDER 3 is inapplicable, Code=BLANK.

Add "2" to the birth order indicated in 04/21-23.
VAR992 "Year pregnancy ended" (YRPREG)

Specifications are identical to the specifications for VAR342 (YRBIRTH).

The only difference is that YRPREG is computed for all pregnancies, regardless
of outcome.

Current pregnancies should be coded blank.

VAR994 "Pregnancy Outcome" (OUTCOME)

This recode assigns a single outcome code to each pregnancy, even if the
pregnancy had multiple outcomes.
47

If the pregnancy outcomes included a live birth (TL 13-14 coded 01,05,07),
it is a live birth, so OUTCOME=1.

If the pregnancy outcomes included an induced abortion but not live birth

(TL 13-14 coded 02 or TL 16 coded 2), it is an induced abortion, so

OUTCOME=2.

If the pregnancy outcomes included a miscarriage but no live births or induced
abortions (TL 13-14 coded 03 or 10 or TL 16 coded 3), then the pregnancy was a
miscarriage, so OUTCOME=3.

If the pregnancy outcomes, included only stillbirths (TL 13-14 coded
04 or TL 16 coded 4), then the pregnancy was a stillbirth, so OUTCOME=4.

If the pregnancy is a current pregnancy (TL 13-14 coded 09), OUTCOME=5.
Missing data: TL 13-14 coded 97,98, or 99.

If the pregnancy did not end in at least one live
birth(04/18-19 coded 02,03,04,06,08,09, or 10),
then the pregnancy was not a live birth, so
YRBIRTH=B1ank.

This is a 2-digit code. Century month of birth (4/25-28) is assigned
a year code as follows:

VAR426 “Wantedness of pregnancy--Wife" (WANTWIFE)

If the pregnancy ended before January 1979(04/25-28 LE 948),
the relevant locations are 09/32-35.
48

If the pregnancy ended in Jan 1979 or later or is a current
pregnancy, (04/25-28=949- )-996 )the relevant locations are
11/72-75.

This recode is written assuming that the pregnancy
ended in Jan 1979 or later. If the pregnancy ended
before Jan 1979, use the analogous locations in 09/31-35.

If the respondent wanted the pregnancy sooner than it
occurred(11/75=2), then the pregnancy was overdue, so
WANTWIFE=1.

If the respondent wanted the pregnancy at about
the time it occurred(11/75=3), then the pregnancy
was on time, so WANTWIFE=2.

If the respondent wanted the pregnancy eventually, but
later than it occurred(11/75=1), then the pregnancy
was mistimed, so WANTWIFE=3.

If the respondent wanted the pregnancy and didn't care
when it occurred(11/75=4), then the respondent was
indifferent, so WANTWIFE=4.

If the respondent did not want to have that pregnancy
ever (11/73=2 or 11/74=2), then the pregnancy
was unwanted, so WANTWIFE=5.

If the respondent didn't know or didn't remember

and probably didn't care about whether she wanted the
pregnancy (11/74=3 or 8), then wantedness

is undetermined, so WANTWIFE=6.

VAR427 "Wantedness of pregnancy--partner" (WANTMAN)

If the pregnancy ended before Jan 1979
(04/25-28 LE 948), the relevant locations are
09/36-37.

If the pregnancy ended in Jan. 1979 or later or is a
current pregnancy, (04/25-28=949-996), the relevant
locations are 11/76-77.

See specifications for VAR 426.

VAR614 "Living arrangements of first or only baby" (LIVBABY1)

For each pregnancy not ending in a live birth (TL 13-14 NE 01,05,07),
code inapplicable=BLANK.

For each pregnancy ending in a live birth (TL 13-14 EQ 01,05,07),
code LIVBABY1 for the first or only baby in the pregnancy using the
specifications for VAR 613 (LIVICHILD).
ey

=r

49

Computation of VAR 615 and VAR 616 repeats specifications for
VAR 614. Blanks assigned if no second or third baby in pregnancy.

VAR620 "Marital status at pregnancy outcome" (MAROUT)

If R is never married (R file TL 11 = 3 or 6), then she was
never married at this pregnancy outcome, code 1

If R is ever married (R file TL 11=1,2,4,5),
if the date the pregnancy ended (TL 20-23) is LT the
date of first marriage (MARIMO,VAR975), she was
never married at this pregnancy outcome, code 1.

if the date the pregnancy ended (TL 20-23) is GE the
date of first marriage (MARIMO,VAR975), she was
ever married at this pregnancy outcome, code 2.
If the pregnancy is a current pregnancy, code blank.

VAR621 "Formal marital status at pregnancy outcome" (FMAROUT)

If R has never formally married (VAR976 eq blank), then she
was never formally married at this pregnancy outcome, code 1.

If R has ever formally married (VAR976 ne blank),
if the date the pregnancy ended (TL 20-23) is LT the
date of first formal marriage (VAR976), she was never
married at this pregnancy outcome, code 1.

if the date the pregnancy ended (TL 20-23) is GE the
date of first formal marriage (VAR976), she was ever
married at this pregnancy outcome, code 2.

If the pregnancy is a current pregnancy, code blank.
